Allow direct reference to the list key as a newline-joined string\n        if prefix:\n            add(prefix, \"\\n\".join(stringify(x) for x in value))\n        for i, item in enumerate(value):\n            child_prefix = f\"{prefix}.{i}\" if prefix else str(i)\n            out.update(flatten_json(item, child_prefix))\n    else:\n        add(prefix, value)\n\n    return out\n\n\ndef stringify(v: Any) -> str:\n    if v is None:\n        return \"\"\n    if isinstance(v, (str, int, float, bool)):\n        return str(v)\n    # Fallback for dict/list: keep deterministic JSON.\n    return json.dumps(v, ensure_ascii=False, separators=(\",\", \":\"))\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ReplacementStats:\n    # placeholder -> count\n    counts: Dict[str, int]\n    # file -> number of replacements\n    file_counts: Dict[str, int]\n    # (file, para_id) for paragraphs where linesegarray was removed\n    lineseg_removed: List[Tuple[str, str]]\n\n\ndef is_text_file(name: str) -> bool:\n    lower = name.lower()\n    return lower.endswith((\".xml\", \".rels\", \".txt\", \".html\", \".htm\"))\n\n\ndef clone_zipinfo(src: zipfile.ZipInfo) -> zipfile.ZipInfo:\n    # Create a new ZipInfo and copy relevant metadata.\n    dst = zipfile.ZipInfo(filename=src.filename, date_time=src.date_time)\n    dst.compress_type = src.compress_type\n    dst.comment = src.comment\n    dst.extra = src.extra\n    dst.create_system = src.create_system\n    dst.create_version = src.create_version\n    dst.extract_version = src.extract_version\n    dst.flag_bits = src.flag_bits\n    dst.internal_attr = src.internal_attr\n    dst.external_attr = src.external_attr\n    dst.volume = getattr(src, \"volume\", 0)\n    return dst\n\n\ndef replace_placeholders_in_text(\n    text: str,\n    mapping: Dict[str, Any],\n    stats: ReplacementStats | None = None,\n    file_name: str | None = None,\n) -> str:\n    def repl(m: re.Match[str]) -> str:\n        key = m.group(1).strip()\n        if key in mapping:\n            val = mapping[key]\n        else:\n            # fallbacks: try raw key as-is, and try stripping surrounding spaces\n            val = mapping.get(key)\n\n        if stats is not None:\n            stats.counts[key] = stats.counts.get(key, 0) + 1\n            if file_name is not None:\n                stats.file_counts[file_name] = stats.file_counts.get(file_name, 0) + 1\n\n        return stringify(val)\n\n    return PLACEHOLDER_RE.sub(repl, text)\n\n\nP_BLOCK_RE = re.compile(r\"(<hp:p\\b[^>]*>.*?</hp:p>)\", re.DOTALL)\n\n\ndef strip_linesegarray(xml_fragment: str) -> str:\n    # Remove both namespaced and unprefixed variants.\n    xml_fragment = re.sub(\n        r\"<hp:linesegarray\\b[^>]*>.*?</hp:linesegarray>\",\n        \"\",\n        xml_fragment,\n        flags=re.DOTALL,\n    )\n    xml_fragment = re.sub(\n        r\"<linesegarray\\b[^>]*>.*?</linesegarray>\",\n        \"\",\n        xml_fragment,\n        flags=re.DOTALL,\n    )\n    return xml_fragment\n\n\ndef get_para_id(p_block: str) -> str:\n    m = re.search(r\"\\bid=\\\"([^\\\"]+)\\\"\", p_block)\n    return m.group(1) if m else \"\"\n\n\ndef render_xml_with_paragraph_sensitive_lineseg_removal(\n    xml: str,\n    mapping: Dict[str, Any],\n    stats: ReplacementStats,\n    file_name: str,\n) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Replace placeholders in an XML string.\n\n    - Replacements are done per <hp:p> block.\n    - If a block changes, strip <*linesegarray> from that block only.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    parts: List[str] = []\n    last = 0\n    for m in P_BLOCK_RE.finditer(xml):\n        start, end = m.span(1)\n        # outside paragraphs: still do placeholder replacement, but don't touch layout caches\n        outside = xml[last:start]\n        if \"{{\" in outside:\n            outside = replace_placeholders_in_text(outside, mapping, stats=stats, file_name=file_name)\n        parts.append(outside)\n\n        p_block = m.group(1)\n        original_block = p_block\n        if \"{{\" in p_block:\n            p_block = replace_placeholders_in_text(p_block, mapping, stats=stats, file_name=file_name)\n\n        if p_block != original_block:\n            para_id = get_para_id(original_block)\n            p_block = strip_linesegarray(p_block)\n            stats.lineseg_removed.append((file_name, para_id))\n\n        parts.append(p_block)\n        last = end\n\n    tail = xml[last:]\n    if \"{{\" in tail:\n        tail = replace_placeholders_in_text(tail, mapping, stats=stats, file_name=file_name)\n    parts.append(tail)\n\n    rendered = \"\".join(parts)\n\n    # Keep both flattened keys and original unflattened top-level keys.\n    mapping.update(flat)\n    if isinstance(data, dict):\n        mapping.update(data)\n\n    stats = ReplacementStats(counts={}, file_counts={}, lineseg_removed=[])\n\n    # Scan placeholders first (for reporting / safety)\n    placeholders: Dict[str, List[str]] = {}\n    with zipfile.ZipFile(TEMPLATE_PATH, \"r\") as zin:\n        for name in zin.namelist():\n            if not is_text_file(name):\n                continue\n            raw = zin.read(name)\n            try:\n                txt = raw.decode(\"utf-8\")\n            except UnicodeDecodeError:\n                continue\n            found = sorted({m.group(1).strip() for m in PLACEHOLDER_RE.finditer(txt)})\n            if found:\n                placeholders[name] = found\n\n    # Render and write output\n    with zipfile.ZipFile(TEMPLATE_PATH, \"r\") as zin, zipfile.ZipFile(OUT_PATH, \"w\") as zout:\n        for name in zin.namelist():\n            info = zin.getinfo(name)\n            src_bytes = zin.read(name)\n            out_bytes = src_bytes\n\n            if is_text_file(name):\n                try:\n                    txt = src_bytes.decode(\"utf-8\")\n                except UnicodeDecodeError:\n                    txt = None\n\n                if txt is not None and (\"{{\" in txt or \"}}\" in txt):\n                    if name.lower().endswith(\".xml\"):\n                        rendered = render_xml_with_paragraph_sensitive_lineseg_removal(\n                            txt, mapping=mapping, stats=stats, file_name=name\n                        )\n                    else:\n                        rendered = replace_placeholders_in_text(txt, mapping, stats=stats, file_name=name)\n\n                    out_bytes = rendered.encode(\"utf-8\")\n\n            out_info = clone_zipinfo(info)\n            zout.writestr(out_info, out_bytes)\n\n    #
